[31m+ const normalizeDefaultVariant = (variants = []) => {[39m
[31m+   if (!Array.isArray(variants) || !variants.length) return [];[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   let defaultIndex = variants.findIndex((variant) => !!variant?.isDefault);[39m

[31m+[39m
[31m+   return variants.map((variant, index) => ({[39m
[31m+     ...variant,[39m
[31m+     isDefault: index === defaultIndex,[39m
[31m+   }));[39m
[31m+ };[39m

[31m+         normalizeVariantNameForCompare(variant?.name) === normalizedName,[39m

[31m+     helper: "Bánh mì, mì, pasta hoặc nguyên liệu từ bột mì.",[39m
[31m+   },[39m
[31m+ ];[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ const FOR_YOU_SUGAR_OPTIONS = [[39m
[31m+   { value: 0, label: "Không đường" },[39m
[31m+   { value: 30, label: "Ít ngọt - 30%" },[39m
[31m+   { value: 50, label: "Ngọt vừa - 50%" },[39m
[31m+   { value: 70, label: "Khá ngọt - 70%" },[39m
[31m+   { value: 100, label: "Mức ngọt tiêu chuẩn - 100%" },[39m
[31m+ ];[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ const FOR_YOU_SPICE_OPTIONS = ["Không", "Vừa", "Nồng", "Rất cay"];[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ const normalizeMenuItemStatus = (status) =>[39m
[31m+   MENU_ITEM_STATUS_SET.has(status) ? status : "available";[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ const buildRecipeForm = (methods = [], existingVariants = []) => {[39m
[31m+   const normalizedMethods = validatePreparationMethods(methods);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return {[39m
[31m+     servingVariants: normalizeDefaultVariant([39m
[31m+       normalizedMethods.map((method, index) => {[39m
[31m+         const existingVariant = findMatchingExistingVariant([39m
[31m+           method,[39m
[31m+           existingVariants,[39m
[31m+         );[39m
[31m+         const fallbackKey =[39m
[31m+           method.name.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, "_") || `sv_${index}`;[39m
[31m+         const mode = method.mode || existingVariant?.mode || "PORTION";[39m
[31m+         const ingredients = existingVariant[39m
[31m+           ? cloneIngredients(existingVariant.ingredients)[39m
[31m+           : Array.isArray(method.ingredients)[39m
[31m+             ? cloneIngredients(method.ingredients)[39m
[31m+             : [];[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         return {[39m
[31m+           key: method.key || existingVariant?.key || fallbackKey,[39m
[31m+           name: method.name,[39m
[31m+           mode,[39m
[31m+           sellQty:[39m
[31m+             Number(method.sellQty ?? existingVariant?.sellQty ?? 1) || 1,[39m
[31m+           sellUnit:[39m
[31m+             method.sellUnit ||[39m
[31m+             existingVariant?.sellUnit ||[39m
[31m+             (mode === "BY_WEIGHT" ? "kg" : "portion"),[39m
[31m+           ingredients,[39m
[31m+           price: method.price,[39m
[31m+           isDefault: !!method.isDefault,[39m
[31m+         };[39m
[31m+       }),[39m
[31m+     ),[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+ };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ const hasVerifiedRecipeData = (recipeItem) =>[39m
[31m+   !!(recipeItem?._rawRecipeId || recipeItem?._rawRecipe);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ const MenuItemModal = ({[39m
[31m+   isOpen,[39m
[31m+   editId,[39m
[31m+   initialFocusSection,[39m
[31m+   categories,[39m
[31m+   menuItems,[39m
[31m+   restaurantId,[39m
[31m+   timeSlot,[39m
[31m+   onSave,[39m
[31m+   onClose,[39m
[31m+ }) => {[39m
[31m+   const [formData, setFormData] = useState({[39m
[31m+     name: "",[39m
[31m+     categoryId: "",[39m
[31m+     status: normalizeMenuItemStatus(),[39m
[31m+     thumbImage: "",[39m
[31m+     description: "",[39m
[31m+     preparationMethods: [],[39m
[31m+     ...FOR_YOU_DEFAULTS,[39m
[31m+   });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const [imageSyncStatus, setImageSyncStatus] = useState("idle");[39m
[31m+   const [toasts, setToasts] = useState([]);[39m
[31m+   const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false);[39m
[31m+   const submitLockRef = useRef(false);[39m
[31m+   const savedMenuItemIdRef = useRef(null);[39m
[31m+   const forYouSectionRef = useRef(null);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     if (!isOpen || !editId || initialFocusSection !== "for-you") {[39m
[31m+       return undefined;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     const timer = window.setTimeout(() => {[39m
[31m+       forYouSectionRef.current?.scrollIntoView({[39m
[31m+         behavior: "smooth",[39m
[31m+         block: "start",[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+       forYouSectionRef.current[39m
[31m+         ?.querySelector("button, input, select, textarea")[39m
[31m+         ?.focus?.();[39m
[31m+     }, 140);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     return () => window.clearTimeout(timer);[39m
[31m+   }, [initialFocusSection, isOpen, editId]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const pushToast = (text, type = "success") => {[39m
[31m+     const id = Date.now();[39m
[31m+     setToasts((current) => [...current, { id, text, type }]);[39m
[31m+     setTimeout([39m
[31m+       () =>[39m
[31m+         setToasts((current) => current.filter((item) => item.id !== id)),[39m
[31m+       3000,[39m
[31m+     );[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const currentItem = useMemo([39m
[31m+     () =>[39m
[31m+       Array.isArray(menuItems) && editId[39m
[31m+         ? menuItems.find((item) => item.id === editId)[39m
[31m+         : null,[39m
[31m+     [menuItems, editId],[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const { createMenuItem, updateMenuItem } = useMenuManagement({[39m
[31m+     restaurantId,[39m
[31m+     defaultTimeSlot: timeSlot,[39m
[31m+     pageSize: 1,[39m
[31m+     useConnection: false,[39m
[31m+   });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const {[39m
[31m+     recipes: recipeItems,[39m
[31m+     updateRecipe,[39m
[31m+     ensureRecipeLoaded,[39m
[31m+     recipeDetailsByMenuItemId,[39m
[31m+   } = useRecipes(restaurantId, timeSlot, {[39m
[31m+     search: null,[39m
[31m+     categoryId: null,[39m
[31m+   });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const currentRecipeItem = useMemo([39m
[31m+     () =>[39m
[31m+       Array.isArray(recipeItems) && editId[39m
[31m+         ? recipeItems.find([39m
[31m+             (item) =>[39m
[31m+               String(item?.menuItemId || item?.id) === String(editId),[39m
[31m+           )[39m
[31m+         : null,[39m
[31m+     [recipeItems, editId],[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const recipeDetailState = useMemo([39m
[31m+     () =>[39m
[31m+       editId ? recipeDetailsByMenuItemId[String(editId)] || null : null,[39m
[31m+     [recipeDetailsByMenuItemId, editId],[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const verifiedCurrentRecipeItem = useMemo([39m
[31m+     () =>[39m
[31m+       hasVerifiedRecipeData(currentRecipeItem) ? currentRecipeItem : null,[39m
[31m+     [currentRecipeItem],[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const verifiedRecipeItem = useMemo(() => {[39m
[31m+     if (verifiedCurrentRecipeItem) return verifiedCurrentRecipeItem;[39m
[31m+     if ([39m
[31m+       recipeDetailState?.status === "loaded" &&[39m
[31m+       hasVerifiedRecipeData(recipeDetailState?.recipe)[39m
[31m+     ) {[39m
[31m+       return recipeDetailState.recipe;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     return null;[39m
[31m+   }, [verifiedCurrentRecipeItem, recipeDetailState]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const existingServingVariants = useMemo([39m
[31m+     () =>[39m
[31m+       Array.isArray(verifiedRecipeItem?.servingVariants)[39m
[31m+         ? verifiedRecipeItem.servingVariants[39m
[31m+         : [],[39m
[31m+     [verifiedRecipeItem],[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const recipeGuardStatus = useMemo(() => {[39m
[31m+     if (!editId) return "ready";[39m
[31m+     if (verifiedRecipeItem) return "ready";[39m
[31m+     if (recipeDetailState?.status === "missing") return "ready";[39m
[31m+     if (recipeDetailState?.status === "error") return "error";[39m
[31m+     return "loading";[39m
[31m+   }, [editId, verifiedRecipeItem, recipeDetailState]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const isRecipeGuardPending =[39m
[31m+     !!editId && recipeGuardStatus === "loading";[39m
[31m+   const isRecipeGuardBlocked = !!editId && recipeGuardStatus === "error";[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const defaultMethod = {[39m
[31m+     key: "",[39m
[31m+     name: "",[39m
[31m+     price: "",[39m
[31m+     cookTime: "",[39m
[31m+     mode: "PORTION",[39m
[31m+     sellQty: 1,[39m
[31m+     sellUnit: "portion",[39m
[31m+     ingredients: [],[39m
[31m+     isDefault: true,[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const isDirty = useMemo(() => {[39m
[31m+     const hasValues =[39m
[31m+       (formData.name || "").trim() ||[39m
[31m+       formData.categoryId ||[39m
[31m+       (formData.description || "").trim() ||[39m
[31m+       (formData.thumbImage || "").trim() ||[39m
[31m+       formData.foodType !== FOR_YOU_DEFAULTS.foodType ||[39m
[31m+       (Array.isArray(formData.meatTypes) && formData.meatTypes.length > 0) ||[39m
[31m+       (Array.isArray(formData.preparationMethods) &&[39m
[31m+         formData.preparationMethods.some([39m
[31m+           (method) =>[39m
[31m+             (method?.name || "").trim() ||[39m
[31m+             method?.price !== "" ||[39m
[31m+             method?.cookTime !== "",[39m
[31m+         ));[39m
[31m+     return !!hasValues;[39m
[31m+   }, [formData]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const { requestCloseWithDraft, clearDraft } = useModalDraft({[39m
[31m+     enabled: isOpen,[39m
[31m+     draftIdentity: {[39m
[31m+       module: "menu",[39m
[31m+       modal: "menu-item-modal",[39m
[31m+       route:[39m
[31m+         typeof window !== "undefined" ? window.location.pathname : "unknown",[39m
[31m+       mode: editId ? "edit" : "create",[39m
[31m+       entityType: "menu-item",[39m
[31m+       recordId: editId || null,[39m
[31m+       context: timeSlot || "all-day",[39m
[31m+       schemaVersion: "1",[39m
[31m+     },[39m
[31m+     formValue: formData,[39m
[31m+     isDirty,[39m
[31m+     sanitize: (value) => ({[39m
[31m+       name: value?.name || "",[39m
[31m+       categoryId: value?.categoryId || "",[39m
[31m+       status: normalizeMenuItemStatus(value?.status),[39m
[31m+       thumbImage: value?.thumbImage || "",[39m
[31m+       description: value?.description || "",[39m
[31m+       foodType: value?.foodType || FOR_YOU_DEFAULTS.foodType,[39m
[31m+       meatTypes: Array.isArray(value?.meatTypes) ? value.meatTypes : [],[39m
[31m+       dietTags: Array.isArray(value?.dietTags) ? value.dietTags : [],[39m
[31m+       allergenTags: Array.isArray(value?.allergenTags)[39m
[31m+         ? value.allergenTags[39m
[31m+         : [],[39m
[31m+       tasteProfile: {[39m
[31m+         ...FOR_YOU_DEFAULTS.tasteProfile,[39m
[31m+         ...(value?.tasteProfile || {}),[39m
[31m+       },[39m
[31m+       preparationMethods: Array.isArray(value?.preparationMethods)[39m
[31m+         ? value.preparationMethods[39m
[31m+         : [],[39m
[31m+     }),[39m
[31m+     onRestore: (draft) =>[39m
[31m+       setFormData((current) => ({ ...current, ...draft })),[39m
[31m+     notify: (message, type) =>[39m
[31m+       pushToast(message, type === "error" ? "error" : "success"),[39m
[31m+   });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     if ([39m
[31m+       !isOpen ||[39m
[31m+       !editId ||[39m
[31m+       !restaurantId ||[39m
[31m+       verifiedCurrentRecipeItem[39m
[31m+     ) {[39m
[31m+       return;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     if ([39m
[31m+       ["loading", "loaded", "missing"].includes(recipeDetailState?.status)[39m
[31m+     ) {[39m
[31m+       return;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     ensureRecipeLoaded(editId);[39m
[31m+   }, [[39m
[31m+     isOpen,[39m
[31m+     editId,[39m
[31m+     restaurantId,[39m
[31m+     verifiedCurrentRecipeItem,[39m
[31m+     recipeDetailState,[39m
[31m+     ensureRecipeLoaded,[39m
[31m+   ]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     if (isOpen) {[39m
[31m+       setImageSyncStatus("idle");[39m
[31m+       setIsSubmitting(false);[39m
[31m+       submitLockRef.current = false;[39m
[31m+       savedMenuItemIdRef.current = editId || null;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       if (editId && currentItem) {[39m
[31m+         const sourceVariants =[39m
[31m+           existingServingVariants.length > 0[39m
[31m+             ? existingServingVariants[39m
[31m+             : Array.isArray(currentItem.servingVariants)[39m
[31m+               ? currentItem.servingVariants[39m
[31m+               : [];[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         const methods =[39m
[31m+           sourceVariants.length > 0[39m
[31m+             ? normalizeDefaultVariant([39m
[31m+                 sourceVariants.map((servingVariant) => ({[39m
[31m+                   key: servingVariant.key || "",[39m
[31m+                   name: servingVariant.name || "",[39m
[31m+                   price:[39m
[31m+                     typeof servingVariant.price === "number"[39m
[31m+                       ? servingVariant.price[39m
[31m+                       : "",[39m
[31m+                   cookTime: currentItem.avgPrepTimeMin || "",[39m
[31m+                   mode: servingVariant.mode || "PORTION",[39m
[31m+                   sellQty: servingVariant.sellQty || 1,[39m
[31m+                   sellUnit: servingVariant.sellUnit || "portion",[39m
[31m+                   ingredients: Array.isArray(servingVariant.ingredients)[39m
[31m+                     ? cloneIngredients(servingVariant.ingredients)[39m
[31m+                     : [],[39m
[31m+                   isDefault: !!servingVariant.isDefault,[39m
[31m+                 })),[39m
[31m+               )[39m
[31m+             : [{ ...defaultMethod }];[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         setFormData({[39m
[31m+           name: currentItem.name || "",[39m
[31m+           categoryId:[39m
[31m+             currentItem.categoryId ||[39m
[31m+             currentItem.category?.id ||[39m
[31m+             currentItem.category ||[39m
[31m+             "",[39m
[31m+           status: normalizeMenuItemStatus(currentItem.status),[39m
[31m+           thumbImage: currentItem.thumbImage || "",[39m
[31m+           description: currentItem.description || "",[39m
[31m+           preparationMethods: methods,[39m
[31m+           foodType: currentItem.foodType || FOR_YOU_DEFAULTS.foodType,[39m
[31m+           meatTypes: Array.isArray(currentItem.meatTypes)[39m
[31m+             ? currentItem.meatTypes[39m
[31m+             : [],[39m
[31m+           dietTags: Array.isArray(currentItem.dietTags)[39m
[31m+             ? currentItem.dietTags[39m
[31m+             : [],[39m
[31m+           allergenTags: Array.isArray(currentItem.allergenTags)[39m
[31m+             ? currentItem.allergenTags[39m
[31m+             : [],[39m
[31m+           tasteProfile: {[39m
[31m+             ...FOR_YOU_DEFAULTS.tasteProfile,[39m
[31m+             ...(currentItem.tasteProfile || {}),[39m
[31m+           },[39m
[31m+         });[39m
[31m+       } else {[39m
[31m+         setFormData({[39m
[31m+           name: "",[39m
[31m+           categoryId: "",[39m
[31m+           status: normalizeMenuItemStatus(),[39m
[31m+           thumbImage: "",[39m
[31m+           description: "",[39m
[31m+           preparationMethods: [{ ...defaultMethod }],[39m
[31m+           ...FOR_YOU_DEFAULTS,[39m
[31m+         });[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+     } else {[39m
[31m+       setIsSubmitting(false);[39m
[31m+       submitLockRef.current = false;[39m
[31m+       savedMenuItemIdRef.current = null;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps[39m
[31m+   }, [editId, currentItem, isOpen]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const handleRequestClose = () => {[39m
[31m+     if (isSubmitting) return;[39m
[31m+     requestCloseWithDraft(onClose);[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const handleInputChange = (field, value) => {[39m
[31m+     setFormData((current) => {[39m
[31m+       if ([39m
[31m+         field === "foodType" &&[39m
[31m+         !["NON_VEGETARIAN", "MIXED"].includes(value)[39m
[31m+       ) {[39m
[31m+         return { ...current, [field]: value, meatTypes: [] };[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+       return { ...current, [field]: value };[39m
[31m+     });[39m
[31m+     if (field === "thumbImage") {[39m
[31m+       setImageSyncStatus("idle");[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const handlePMChange = (index, field, value) => {[39m
[31m+     setFormData((current) => ({[39m
[31m+       ...current,[39m
[31m+       preparationMethods: current.preparationMethods.map([39m
[31m+         (method, currentIndex) =>[39m
[31m+           currentIndex === index ? { ...method, [field]: value } : method,[39m
[31m+       ),[39m
[31m+     }));[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const toggleArrayValue = (field, value) => {[39m
[31m+     setFormData((current) => {[39m
[31m+       const values = Array.isArray(current[field]) ? current[field] : [];[39m
[31m+       return {[39m
[31m+         ...current,[39m
[31m+         [field]: values.includes(value)[39m
[31m+           ? values.filter((item) => item !== value)[39m
[31m+           : [...values, value],[39m
[31m+       };[39m
[31m+     });[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const addPM = () => {[39m
[31m+     if (isSubmitting) return;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     setFormData((current) => ({[39m
[31m+       ...current,[39m
[31m+       preparationMethods: [[39m
[31m+         ...current.preparationMethods,[39m
[31m+         { ...defaultMethod, isDefault: false },[39m
[31m+       ],[39m
[31m+     }));[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const removePM = (index) => {[39m
[31m+     if (isSubmitting) return;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     if (formData.preparationMethods.length > 1) {[39m
[31m+       setFormData((current) => ({[39m
[31m+         ...current,[39m
[31m+         preparationMethods: current.preparationMethods.filter([39m
[31m+           (_, currentIndex) => currentIndex !== index,[39m
[31m+         ),[39m
[31m+       }));[39m
[31m+     } else {[39m
[31m+       pushToast("Món cần có ít nhất một cách chế biến.", "error");[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const handleRetryRecipeLoad = async () => {[39m
[31m+     if (!editId || isSubmitting || isRecipeGuardPending) return;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     const nextState = await ensureRecipeLoaded(editId);[39m
[31m+     if (nextState?.status === "error") {[39m
[31m+       pushToast([39m
[31m+         `Không thể tải định lượng nguyên liệu: ${getGraphQLErrorMessage([39m
[31m+           nextState.error,[39m
[31m+           "Vui lòng thử lại.",[39m
[31m+         )}`,[39m
[31m+         "error",[39m
[31m+       );[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const handleSubmit = async (event) => {[39m
[31m+     event.preventDefault();[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     if (submitLockRef.current) return;[39m
[31m+     if (isRecipeGuardPending) {[39m
[31m+       pushToast([39m
[31m+         "Đang tải định lượng nguyên liệu. Vui lòng chờ thêm một chút.",[39m
[31m+         "error",[39m
[31m+       );[39m
[31m+       return;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     if (isRecipeGuardBlocked) {[39m
[31m+       await handleRetryRecipeLoad();[39m
[31m+       return;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     if (!restaurantId) {[39m
[31m+       pushToast([39m
[31m+         "Không xác định được nhà hàng. Vui lòng chọn lại nhà hàng.",[39m
[31m+         "error",[39m
[31m+       );[39m
[31m+       return;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     const itemName = String(formData.name || "").trim();[39m
[31m+     if (!itemName) {[39m
[31m+       pushToast("Vui lòng nhập tên món ăn.", "error");[39m
[31m+       return;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     const categoryId = String(formData.categoryId || "").trim();[39m
[31m+     if (!categoryId) {[39m
[31m+       pushToast("Vui lòng chọn danh mục món.", "error");[39m
[31m+       return;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     let normalizedMethods;[39m
[31m+     try {[39m
[31m+       normalizedMethods = validatePreparationMethods([39m
[31m+         formData.preparationMethods,[39m
[31m+       );[39m
[31m+     } catch (error) {[39m
[31m+       pushToast(error.message, "error");[39m
[31m+       return;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     const cookTimes = normalizedMethods[39m
[31m+       .map((method) => parseInt(method.cookTime, 10))[39m
[31m+       .filter((value) => Number.isFinite(value) && value >= 0);[39m
[31m+     const avgPrepTimeMin =[39m
[31m+       cookTimes.length > 0[39m
[31m+         ? Math.round([39m
[31m+             cookTimes.reduce((sum, value) => sum + value, 0) /[39m
[31m+               cookTimes.length,[39m
[31m+           )[39m
[31m+         : undefined;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     submitLockRef.current = true;[39m
[31m+     setIsSubmitting(true);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     try {[39m
[31m+       const menuItemPayload = {[39m
[31m+         name: itemName,[39m
[31m+         categoryId,[39m
[31m+         status: normalizeMenuItemStatus(formData.status),[39m
[31m+         description: formData.description,[39m
[31m+         ...(Number.isFinite(avgPrepTimeMin) ? { avgPrepTimeMin } : {}),[39m
[31m+         ...(formData.thumbImage?.trim()[39m
[31m+           ? { thumbImage: formData.thumbImage.trim() }[39m
[31m+           : {}),[39m
[31m+         dietTags: Array.from([39m
[31m+           new Set((formData.dietTags || []).filter(Boolean)),[39m
[31m+         ),[39m
[31m+         allergenTags: Array.from([39m
[31m+           new Set((formData.allergenTags || []).filter(Boolean)),[39m
[31m+         ),[39m
[31m+         foodType: formData.foodType || FOR_YOU_DEFAULTS.foodType,[39m
[31m+         meatTypes: ["NON_VEGETARIAN", "MIXED"].includes([39m
[31m+           formData.foodType,[39m
[31m+         )[39m
[31m+           ? Array.from(new Set((formData.meatTypes || []).filter(Boolean)))[39m
[31m+           : [],[39m
[31m+         tasteProfile: {[39m
[31m+           ...FOR_YOU_DEFAULTS.tasteProfile,[39m
[31m+           ...(formData.tasteProfile || {}),[39m
[31m+         },[39m
[31m+       };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       let targetMenuItemId =[39m
[31m+         editId || savedMenuItemIdRef.current || null;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       try {[39m
[31m+         if (targetMenuItemId) {[39m
[31m+           await updateMenuItem({[39m
[31m+             id: targetMenuItemId,[39m
[31m+             ...menuItemPayload,[39m
[31m+           });[39m
[31m+         } else {[39m
[31m+           const created = await createMenuItem({[39m
[31m+             ...menuItemPayload,[39m
[31m+             timeSlot,[39m
[31m+           });[39m
[31m+           targetMenuItemId = created?.id;[39m
[31m+           savedMenuItemIdRef.current = targetMenuItemId || null;[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+       } catch (error) {[39m
[31m+         pushToast(getGraphQLErrorMessage(error), "error");[39m
[31m+         return;[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       if (!targetMenuItemId) {[39m
[31m+         pushToast([39m
[31m+           "Không thể xác nhận món vừa lưu. Vui lòng thử lại.",[39m
[31m+           "error",[39m
[31m+         );[39m
[31m+         return;[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       try {[39m
[31m+         const recipeForm = buildRecipeForm([39m
[31m+           normalizedMethods,[39m
[31m+           existingServingVariants,[39m
[31m+         );[39m
[31m+         await updateRecipe(targetMenuItemId, recipeForm);[39m
[31m+       } catch (error) {[39m
[31m+         const actionLabel = editId ? "cập nhật" : "tạo";[39m
[31m+         pushToast([39m
[31m+           `Món đã được ${actionLabel}, nhưng cách chế biến hoặc định lượng nguyên liệu chưa lưu được: ${getGraphQLErrorMessage([39m
[31m+             error,[39m
[31m+             "Không thể lưu định lượng nguyên liệu.",[39m
[31m+           )}`,[39m
[31m+           "error",[39m
[31m+         );[39m
[31m+         return;[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       try {[39m
[31m+         await onSave?.();[39m
[31m+       } catch (error) {[39m
[31m+         pushToast([39m
[31m+           `Đã lưu món và định lượng nguyên liệu nhưng chưa thể làm mới danh sách: ${getGraphQLErrorMessage([39m
[31m+             error,[39m
[31m+             "Không thể tải lại dữ liệu.",[39m
[31m+           )}`,[39m
[31m+           "error",[39m
[31m+         );[39m
[31m+         return;[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       clearDraft();[39m
[31m+       pushToast([39m
[31m+         editId[39m
[31m+           ? "Đã lưu thay đổi món ăn."[39m
[31m+           : "Đã tạo món mới.",[39m
[31m+         "success",[39m
[31m+       );[39m
[31m+     } finally {[39m
[31m+       submitLockRef.current = false;[39m
[31m+       setIsSubmitting(false);[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const isSaving = isSubmitting;[39m
[31m+   const isSubmitDisabled = isSaving || isRecipeGuardPending;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const recipeTrackingStatus = useMemo(() => {[39m
[31m+     const methods = Array.isArray(formData.preparationMethods)[39m
[31m+       ? formData.preparationMethods[39m
[31m+       : [];[39m
[31m+     const hasMethods =[39m
[31m+       methods.length > 0 &&[39m
[31m+       methods.some((method) => String(method?.name || "").trim());[39m
[31m+     const hasIngredients = methods.some([39m
[31m+       (method) =>[39m
[31m+         Array.isArray(method?.ingredients) && method.ingredients.length > 0,[39m
[31m+     );[39m
[31m+     if (hasIngredients) return "tracked";[39m
[31m+     if (hasMethods) return "missing_ingredients";[39m
[31m+     return "not_tracked";[39m
[31m+   }, [formData.preparationMethods]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const dietLabelMap = useMemo([39m
[31m+     () =>[39m
[31m+       new Map([39m
[31m+         FOR_YOU_DIET_OPTIONS.map((option) => [[39m
[31m+           option.value,[39m
[31m+           option.label,[39m
[31m+         ]),[39m
[31m+       ),[39m
[31m+     [],[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const allergenLabelMap = useMemo([39m
[31m+     () =>[39m
[31m+       new Map([39m
[31m+         FOR_YOU_ALLERGEN_OPTIONS.map((option) => [[39m
[31m+           option.value,[39m
[31m+           option.label,[39m
[31m+         ]),[39m
[31m+       ),[39m
[31m+     [],[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const selectedDietLabels = (formData.dietTags || []).map([39m
[31m+     (tag) => dietLabelMap.get(tag) || tag,[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+   const selectedAllergenLabels = (formData.allergenTags || []).map([39m
[31m+     (tag) => allergenLabelMap.get(tag) || tag,[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+   const foodTypeLabel =[39m
[31m+     FOOD_TYPE_OPTIONS.find((option) => option.value === formData.foodType)[39m
[31m+       ?.label || FOOD_TYPE_OPTIONS[0].label;[39m
[31m+   const selectedMeatLabels = (formData.meatTypes || []).map([39m
[31m+     (type) =>[39m
[31m+       MEAT_TYPE_OPTIONS.find((option) => option.value === type)?.label || type,[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+   const shouldShowMeatTypes = ["NON_VEGETARIAN", "MIXED"].includes([39m
[31m+     formData.foodType,[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+   const currentSugarValue = Number(formData.tasteProfile?.sugar ?? 100);[39m
[31m+   const sugarPreviewLabel =[39m
[31m+     FOR_YOU_SUGAR_OPTIONS.find([39m
[31m+       (option) => option.value === currentSugarValue,[39m
[31m+     )?.label || `${currentSugarValue}%`;[39m
[31m+   const spicePreviewLabel = formData.tasteProfile?.spice ?? "Vừa";[39m
[31m+   const tasteNotes = [[39m
[31m+     formData.tasteProfile?.containsOnion ? "Có hành" : null,[39m
[31m+     formData.tasteProfile?.containsCilantro ? "Có ngò (rau mùi)" : null,[39m
[31m+     `Mức ngọt: ${sugarPreviewLabel}`,[39m
[31m+     `Mức cay: ${spicePreviewLabel}`,[39m
[31m+   ].filter(Boolean);[39m
[31m+   const hasForYouMetadata =[39m
[31m+     selectedDietLabels.length > 0 ||[39m
[31m+     selectedAllergenLabels.length > 0 ||[39m
[31m+     formData.foodType !== FOR_YOU_DEFAULTS.foodType ||[39m
[31m+     selectedMeatLabels.length > 0 ||[39m
[31m+     !!formData.tasteProfile?.containsOnion ||[39m
[31m+     !!formData.tasteProfile?.containsCilantro ||[39m
[31m+     currentSugarValue !== FOR_YOU_DEFAULTS.tasteProfile.sugar ||[39m
[31m+     spicePreviewLabel !== FOR_YOU_DEFAULTS.tasteProfile.spice;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <Modal[39m
[31m+       isOpen={isOpen}[39m
[31m+       onClose={handleRequestClose}[39m
[31m+       size="xl"[39m
[31m+       className="menu-item-modal-modern"[39m
[31m+     >[39m
[31m+       <Modal.Header onClose={handleRequestClose}>[39m
[31m+         {editId ? "Chỉnh sửa món ăn" : "Thêm món mới"}[39m

[31m+       <Modal.Body>[39m

[31m+           onSubmit={handleSubmit}[39m

[31m+         >[39m
[31m+           <div className="left-col">[39m
[31m+             <h4 className="col-title">[39m
[31m+               <Info size={18} /> Thông tin món ăn[39m
[31m+             </h4>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+             <div className="form-group">[39m
[31m+               <label>[39m

[31m+               </label>[39m
[31m+               <input[39m
[31m+                 type="text"[39m
[31m+                 className="modern-input"[39m
[31m+                 value={formData.name}[39m
[31m+                 onChange={(event) =>[39m
[31m+                   handleInputChange("name", event.target.value)[39m
[31m+                 }[39m
[31m+                 placeholder="Ví dụ: Phở bò tái"[39m
[31m+                 required[39m
[31m+                 autoFocus[39m
[31m+                 disabled={isSaving}[39m
[31m+               />[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+             <div className="row-2-col">[39m
[31m+               <div className="form-group">[39m
[31m+                 <label>[39m
[31m+                   Danh mục món <span className="req">*</span>[39m
[31m+                 </label>[39m
[31m+                 <select[39m
[31m+                   className="modern-select"[39m

[31m+                   {categories?.map((category) => ([39m
[31m+                     <option[39m
[31m+                       key={category.id || category._id}[39m

[31m+                   value={formData.status}[39m
[31m+                   onChange={(event) =>[39m
[31m+                     handleInputChange("status", event.target.value)[39m
[31m+                   }[39m
[31m+                   disabled={isSaving}[39m
[31m+                 >[39m
[31m+                   {MENU_ITEM_STATUS_OPTIONS.map((statusOption) => ([39m
[31m+                     <option[39m
[31m+                       key={statusOption.value}[39m
[31m+                       value={statusOption.value}[39m

[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+[39m

[31m+               <label>Ảnh món ăn</label>[39m
[31m+               <LocalImagePicker[39m
[31m+                 value={formData.thumbImage || ""}[39m
[31m+                 onChange={(value) =>[39m
[31m+                   handleInputChange("thumbImage", value)[39m
[31m+                 }[39m
[31m+                 disabled={isSaving}[39m
[31m+                 ownerKey={[39m
[31m+                   editId ||[39m
[31m+                   savedMenuItemIdRef.current ||[39m
[31m+                   restaurantId ||[39m

[31m+               />[39m
[31m+               {(imageSyncStatus === "localOnly" ||[39m
[31m+                 getImagePersistenceStatus(formData.thumbImage) ===[39m

[31m+                 getImagePersistenceStatus(formData.thumbImage) ===[39m

[31m+                   style={{[39m

[31m+[39m
[31m+             <div className="form-group">[39m
[31m+               <label>Mô tả món</label>[39m

[31m+                 className="modern-textarea"[39m
[31m+                 rows="4"[39m
[31m+                 value={formData.description}[39m

[31m+                           {option.helper}[39m
[31m+                         </span>[39m
[31m+                       </label>[39m
[31m+                     );[39m
